## Routes, roles and navigation

### NAV-001 — Public application routes

- **Feature area:** Routes, roles and navigation. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** partial. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest, User, Creator and Admin as parameterized. Published fixtures for each product type and a Creator storefront.
- **Steps:** Direct-load and reload /app/explore, /app/explore/search?term=sample, /app/product/{id}, /app/product/{id}/{type}, /app/store/{creatorId} as each role.
- **Expected results:** Routes are not role-guarded; public data and actions follow the feature-specific cases below, without granting protected content.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.
- **Sources:** [App.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/App.tsx); [app-router.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/routes/app-router.tsx); [marketing-router.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/marketing/routes/marketing-router.tsx); [auth-router.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/routes/auth-router.tsx); [protected-route.util.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/providers/protected-route.util.tsx); [app-layout.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/pages/app-layout/app-layout.component.tsx); [routes.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/constants/routes.ts)

### NAV-002 — Guest protected-route gate

- **Feature area:** Routes, roles and navigation. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** partial. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest, User, Creator and Admin as parameterized. Guest with no valid session; existing IDs for nested routes.
- **Steps:** Open /app, /app/settings, /app/cart, /app/products, /app/products/create, /app/products/edit/{id}, /app/products/edit/{type}/{id}, /app/products/{id}, /app/products/{id}/landing-page, /app/products/{id}/preview, /app/customers, /app/customers/{id}, /app/sales, /app/analytics, /app/storefront, /app/admin and its users/products/products/create/audit children, /app/library and its five children, /app/my-page-preview, /onboarding.
- **Expected results:** After authentication resolves false, redirect to /auth/login; no protected page data should be rendered. Initial null-auth behavior is investigated separately.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.
- **Sources:** [App.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/App.tsx); [app-router.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/routes/app-router.tsx); [marketing-router.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/marketing/routes/marketing-router.tsx); [auth-router.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/routes/auth-router.tsx); [protected-route.util.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/providers/protected-route.util.tsx); [app-layout.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/pages/app-layout/app-layout.component.tsx); [routes.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/constants/routes.ts)

### NAV-003 — User route authorization

- **Feature area:** Routes, roles and navigation. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** partial. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest, User, Creator and Admin as parameterized. Signed-in User.
- **Steps:** Open /app/settings, /app/cart and /app/library/{all-products,my-courses,my-download-packages,my-consultation,my-wishlist}; then open management/admin/customer/report/storefront routes listed in NAV-002.
- **Expected results:** Allowed settings/cart/library routes render. Management routes deny with /unauthorized; that page labels the denial “401 - Unauthorized” and provides Login.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.

### NAV-008 — Authentication loading and null state

## Registration, sign-in and account recovery

### AUTH-001 — Registration happy path

- **Feature area:** Registration, sign-in and account recovery. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest with isolated synthetic account fixtures. Unused synthetic email; first name 8–50 characters, last name 1–50, password length 8–64 and matching confirmation; valid CSRF prerequisite if required.
- **Steps:** Open /auth/signup; fill fields; submit once; inspect registration response and email-sent route; attempt sign-in before verification.
- **Expected results:** Registration creates a disabled USER with onboarding incomplete and a verification email request; browser routes to /auth/email-sent. Sign-in is rejected until verification.
- **Required environment / external service:** Frontend and backend; synthetic credentials supplied privately by fixtures; SendGrid test mailbox for email flows.
- **Sources:** [auth-api.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/services/auth/auth-api.ts); [auth.slice.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/store/auth-store/auth.slice.ts); [AuthController.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/controller/auth/AuthController.java); [AuthService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/AuthService.java); [RegisterRequest.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/dto/authetication/RegisterRequest.java); [LoginRequest.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/dto/authetication/LoginRequest.java); [VerificationTokenService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/VerificationTokenService.java); [sign-up.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/pages/sign-up/sign-up.component.tsx); [sign-in.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/pages/sign-in/sign-in.component.tsx); [verify-email.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/pages/verify-email/verify-email.component.tsx); [email-sent.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/pages/email-sent/email-sent.component.tsx); [forgot-password.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/pages/forgot-password/forgot-password.component.tsx); [enter-code.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/features/password-change/enter-code/enter-code.component.tsx); [change-password.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/features/password-change/change-password/change-password.component.tsx)

### AUTH-002 — Registration client validation

- **Feature area:** Registration, sign-in and account recovery. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest with isolated synthetic account fixtures. Blank fields, malformed email and unequal confirmation variants.
- **Steps:** Submit each invalid variant on /auth/signup; correct the indicated fields.
- **Expected results:** Submission stops on required/email/matching validation; no registration is sent for those invalid variants.
- **Required environment / external service:** Frontend and backend; synthetic credentials supplied privately by fixtures; SendGrid test mailbox for email flows.

### AUTH-003 — Registration name and credential boundary conflict

- **Feature area:** Registration, sign-in and account recovery. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest with isolated synthetic account fixtures. First name lengths 1,7,8,50,51; last name 0,1,50,51; password lengths 1,7,8,64,65.
- **Steps:** Submit the browser form and separately POST /api/auth/register using each boundary.
- **Expected results:** Backend requires first name 8–50 despite its message, last name 1–50 and password 8–64; invalid DTOs return 400 field errors. expected result needs investigation for browser error presentation because submit has no rejection rendering and password rules are not fully enforced client-side.
- **Required environment / external service:** Frontend and backend; synthetic credentials supplied privately by fixtures; SendGrid test mailbox for email flows.
- **Sources:** [auth-api.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/services/auth/auth-api.ts); [auth.slice.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/store/auth-store/auth.slice.ts); [AuthController.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/controller/auth/AuthController.java); [AuthService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/AuthService.java); [RegisterRequest.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/dto/authetication/RegisterRequest.java); [LoginRequest.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/dto/authetication/LoginRequest.java); [VerificationTokenService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/VerificationTokenService.java); [sign-up.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/pages/sign-up/sign-up.component.tsx); [sign-in.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/pages/sign-in/sign-in.component.tsx); [verify-email.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/pages/verify-email/verify-email.component.tsx); [email-sent.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/pages/email-sent/email-sent.component.tsx); [forgot-password.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/auth/pages/forgot-password/forgot-password.component.tsx); [enter-code.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/features/password-change/enter-code/enter-code.component.tsx); [change-password.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/features/password-change/change-password/change-password.component.tsx)

### AUTH-004 — Registration whitespace and duplicate

- **Feature area:** Registration, sign-in and account recovery. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest with isolated synthetic account fixtures. Existing synthetic email; all-space names; repeated submission while pending.
- **Steps:** POST /api/auth/register for duplicate email; submit whitespace names and double-submit a fresh signup separately.
- **Expected results:** Duplicate is rejected by authentication logic; expected result needs investigation for whitespace-only name acceptance and pending-submit duplication because UI uses truthiness and backend uses size constraints.
- **Required environment / external service:** Frontend and backend; synthetic credentials supplied privately by fixtures; SendGrid test mailbox for email flows.

### AUTH-006 — Verification valid token

## Session, API security and feature flags

### SEC-001 — Protected endpoint authentication

- **Feature area:** Session, API security and feature flags. **Test level:** API integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P0 — authentication or data boundary. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest and authenticated role fixtures. No JWT cookie; valid test entity IDs. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.
- **Steps:** GET /api/user/userInfo, /api/entitlements/me, /api/commerce/orders/{id}, /api/admin/users and /; repeat with expired/malformed JWT cookies.
- **Expected results:** No private data is returned; valid cookies are required. Exact filter-originated error body/status needs investigation where exceptions occur before controller advice.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.
- **Sources:** [SecurityConfig.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/configs/SecurityConfig.java); [CsrfFilter.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/CsrfFilter.java); [JwtAuthenticationFilter.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/JwtAuthenticationFilter.java); [AuthService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/AuthService.java); [http-client.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/http-client.ts); [csrf.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/interceptors/csrf.ts); [refresh-token.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/interceptors/refresh-token.ts)

Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.
- **Steps:** GET /testEndpoint, /api/products/get-all-products-min, /api/products/search?term=sample, /api/products/{publishedId}, /api/storefronts/{creatorId}, /api/calendars/providers.

### SEC-003 — Cookie versus bearer authentication

- **Feature area:** Session, API security and feature flags. **Test level:** API integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P0 — authentication or data boundary. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest and authenticated role fixtures. Valid synthetic JWT available to test harness. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.
- **Steps:** Call GET /api/user/userInfo with only Authorization bearer header, then with valid JWT cookie.
- **Expected results:** JWT filter uses cookie authentication; header alone does not create an authenticated principal.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.
- **Sources:** [SecurityConfig.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/configs/SecurityConfig.java); [CsrfFilter.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/CsrfFilter.java); [JwtAuthenticationFilter.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/JwtAuthenticationFilter.java); [AuthService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/AuthService.java); [http-client.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/http-client.ts); [csrf.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/interceptors/csrf.ts); [refresh-token.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/interceptors/refresh-token.ts)

### SEC-004 — CSRF matching pair

- **Feature area:** Session, API security and feature flags. **Test level:** API integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P0 — authentication or data boundary. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest and authenticated role fixtures. Authenticated role with issued XSRF cookie. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.
- **Steps:** PUT /api/user/userInfo with matching X-XSRF-TOKEN header; repeat with missing header, mismatch, and missing cookie.
- **Expected results:** Matching pair permits normal validation; missing/mismatched pair rejects mutation without saving. Auth login/logout/refresh/google exclusions are separate from register.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.
- **Sources:** [SecurityConfig.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/configs/SecurityConfig.java); [CsrfFilter.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/CsrfFilter.java); [JwtAuthenticationFilter.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/JwtAuthenticationFilter.java); [AuthService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/security/AuthService.java); [http-client.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/http-client.ts); [csrf.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/interceptors/csrf.ts); [refresh-token.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/interceptors/refresh-token.ts)

### SEC-005 — Frontend mutation header interoperability

- **Feature area:** Session, API security and feature flags. **Test level:** API integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P0 — authentication or data boundary. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest and authenticated role fixtures. Signed-in browser; canonical and leading-slash client URLs. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.

- **Expected results:** expected result needs investigation for exclusion matching because interceptors match slash-prefixed strings while clients mix URL forms. Correctly attached token must permit supported mutations.

### SEC-013 — Secure cookies across deployed origins

- **Feature area:** Session, API security and feature flags. **Test level:** deployed-service integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P0 — authentication or data boundary. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest and authenticated role fixtures. Issued session and approved configured frontend origin.
- **Steps:** Sign in over HTTPS; inspect cookie attributes without recording values; reload and perform mutation; repeat with browser third-party-cookie restrictions.
- **Expected results:** JWT/refresh are HttpOnly, Secure, SameSite=None; XSRF is readable and Secure; configured domain/path must allow browser requests. Access token lifetime differs from cookie max-age, so expiry invokes refresh. Cross-site restrictions may prevent session establishment.
- **Required environment / external service:** HTTPS deployment using approved test origins and browser cookie-policy variants.

### SEC-015 — Mock flag does not establish complete mocked app

- **Feature area:** Session, API security and feature flags. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** partial. **Priority:** P0 — authentication or data boundary. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Guest and authenticated role fixtures. Frontend builds with REACT_APP_USE_MOCKS false and true; isolated network denied by harness.

## Profile, onboarding and Settings

### PROF-001 — Onboarding field steps

- **Feature area:** Profile, onboarding and Settings. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** partial. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Authenticated User, Creator or Admin. New profile with incomplete onboarding.
- **Steps:** Use Welcome → Basic → About → Additional → summary; enter title, bio, tagline, website and synthetic location/social data; advance and reload profile.
- **Expected results:** Profile updates are sent after initial step; valid saved fields should reload. Requests are not awaited before advancing, so save-failure feedback and partially completed progress need investigation.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.

### PROD-009 — Create required input and duplicate click

- **Feature area:** Product management, workspace and lifecycle. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Creator; authenticated owner with synthetic products. Empty name or missing type; delayed valid create response.

## Legacy Course authoring API

### LEG-001 — Legacy Course section lifecycle

- **Feature area:** Legacy Course authoring API. **Test level:** API integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Creator owner and Admin; valid cookie and CSRF. Disposable Course; body satisfies legacy DTO including productId/userId where declared. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.
- **Steps:** POST /api/products/course/section; retain returned ID; PUT same path with updated fields; DELETE same path?userId={caller}&id={sectionId}; GET Product after each.
- **Expected results:** Create201, update/delete200 text; section persists then removes using actual parent ownership, not supplied userId alone.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.

- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Creator owner for authoring, entitled User for submission; valid role cookies and CSRF. Owned QUIZ lesson; User ACTIVE access to parent Course.
- **Steps:** PUT /api/lessons/{l}/quiz with {"passingScore":80,"questions":[{"title":"Select A","type":"multiple_choice_single","points":5,"options":[{"text":"A","isCorrect":true,"position":1},{"text":"B","isCorrect":false,"position":2}],"position":1,"shuffle":false}]}; GET play as learner; POST submit with {"answers":[{"questionId":"{returnedQuestionId}","selectedOptionIds":["{returnedOptionAId}"]}]}.
- **Expected results:** Author response supplies stable server IDs; play omits correctness; valid correct submission scores5/5 and100percent with successful pass outcome; this is a proposed API case, not an executed learner test.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.
- **Sources:** [QuizDraftDto.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/dto/products/course/quiz/QuizDraftDto.java); [QuizQuestionDto.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/dto/products/course/quiz/QuizQuestionDto.java); [QuizOptionDto.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/dto/products/course/quiz/QuizOptionDto.java); [QuizSubmissionRequest.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/dto/products/course/quiz/QuizSubmissionRequest.java); [QuizSubmissionAnswerDto.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/dto/products/course/quiz/QuizSubmissionAnswerDto.java); [LessonQuizController.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/controller/product/course/quiz/LessonQuizController.java); [QuizSubmissionEvaluator.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/product/course/quiz/QuizSubmissionEvaluator.java)

## Download upload, metadata and delivery preparation

### FILE-001 — Canonical upload lifecycle

- **Feature area:** Download upload, metadata and delivery preparation. **Test level:** deployed-service integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Creator owner/Admin; disposable Download Product and section. Small synthetic file with distinctive filename and bytes.
- **Steps:** Select file in Download section; obtain GET /api/products/{p}/sections/{s}/files/presigned-url?filename={name}; PUT bytes to returned presignedUrl; POST confirm-upload; reload workspace.
- **Expected results:** File appears only after lifecycle completes;201 confirmation returns persisted metadata; Product read suppresses permanent file URL. Actual byte delivery is separate entitlement flow.
- **Required environment / external service:** Frontend/backend plus isolated DigitalOcean Spaces bucket and permitted CORS; synthetic non-sensitive files.
- **Sources:** [section-editor.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/features/product-form/editors/section-editor/section-editor.component.tsx); [product-download-files-api.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/services/products/product-download-files-api.ts); [upload-lifecycle.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/services/uploads/upload-lifecycle.ts); [ProductDownloadFilesController.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/controller/product/download/ProductDownloadFilesController.java); [DownloadFilesController.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/controller/product/download/DownloadFilesController.java); [FilesService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/product/FilesService.java); [ConfirmUploadRequestDto.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/dto/s3_files/ConfirmUploadRequestDto.java); [SpacesS3Service.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/digitalocean/SpacesS3Service.java)

- **Feature area:** Download upload, metadata and delivery preparation. **Test level:** deployed-service integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.

- **Steps:** Trigger browser thumbnail/gallery/promo upload; compare transmitted Content-Type/body with raw-stream controller contract.
- **Expected results:** expected result needs investigation: client inherits application/json default while submitting File; do not assume server MIME validation receives actual media MIME.

## Consultation details and weekly availability

### CONS-001 — Details round-trip

- **Feature area:** Consultation details and weekly availability. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Creator owner/Admin; Consultation draft. Duration30/45/50/60/75/90/120; ZOOM/GOOGLE_MEET/PHONE/OTHER fixtures; valid weekly window.
- **Steps:** Set duration, method, location where needed, buffers, daily limit, confirmation message and policy; wait save; reload.
- **Expected results:** Supported metadata persists in Consultation details; meeting method does not create an external room/event.

### ACCESS-001 — Free enrollment API matrix

Published COURSE/DOWNLOAD with price null or zero; legacy preseeded free CONSULTATION; no existing entitlement; valid session/CSRF. ### ACCESS-013 — Download authorized URL contract

- **Feature area:** Entitlements, Library and protected delivery. **Test level:** deployed-service integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P0 — paid or private content access. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Authenticated User; Creator/Admin API variants explicitly included. ACTIVE holder, owner and Admin variants; stored synthetic file.
- **Steps:** GET entitlement download endpoint; inspect expiry metadata without retaining URL; use URL in isolated browser.
- **Expected results:** Short-lived URL valid for tenminutes; access path increments download count; service does not return permanent public link.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend/backend and isolated DigitalOcean Spaces bucket; signed URLs retained only privately by harness.

### ACCESS-014 — Download expiration and retry

- **Feature area:** Entitlements, Library and protected delivery. **Test level:** deployed-service integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P0 — paid or private content access. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Authenticated User; Creator/Admin API variants explicitly included. Expired signed URL; still-active entitlement.
- **Steps:** Use expired URL; request a fresh one and download again.

### ACCESS-015 — Download browser recovery

- **Feature area:** Entitlements, Library and protected delivery. **Test level:** deployed-service integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P0 — paid or private content access. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Authenticated User; Creator/Admin API variants explicitly included. Entitled Product; endpoint failure and missing object fixtures.

### CART-014 — Checkout URL handoff and missing session identity

- **Feature area:** Cart, wishlist and browser checkout. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** partial. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** User; Guest local collection variants and Creator/Admin Cart routes. Controlled response with checkoutUrl; separately response missing orderId and URL.
- **Steps:** Start paid checkout in isolated harness.

## One-time orders and payment-state integration

### PAY-001 — Commerce disabled gate

- **Feature area:** One-time orders and payment-state integration. **Test level:** API integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P0 — financial or entitlement integrity. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Authenticated synthetic buyer; Creator owner different from buyer; CSRF on every POST. COMMERCE_ENABLED false or enabled without available gateway. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.
- **Steps:** POST /api/commerce/checkout-sessions with valid productIds and Idempotency-Key.
- **Expected results:** 503 deliberate unavailable response; no paid order/access fabricated.
- **Required environment / external service:** Isolated backend with persistent test data; explicit commerce/fake configuration per case; no real charges.
- **Sources:** [CommerceController.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/controller/commerce/CommerceController.java); [CommerceCheckoutRequest.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/dto/commerce/CommerceCheckoutRequest.java); [CommerceCheckoutService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/commerce/CommerceCheckoutService.java); [CommerceOrderQueryService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/commerce/CommerceOrderQueryService.java); [CommerceOrderMapper.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/commerce/CommerceOrderMapper.java); [CommercePaymentEventService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/commerce/CommercePaymentEventService.java); [DevCommerceController.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/controller/commerce/DevCommerceController.java); [FakePaymentGateway.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/commerce/payment/FakePaymentGateway.java)

### PAY-002 — Paid session creation

Commerce enabled/provider fake/auto-success false;1 and20 unique published positive-price Course/Download/Consultation from one Creator. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.

### PAY-005 — Idempotency stable replay

- **Feature area:** One-time orders and payment-state integration. **Test level:** API integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P0 — financial or entitlement integrity. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Authenticated synthetic buyer; Creator owner different from buyer; CSRF on every POST. Existing order; same buyer and key; reorder identical IDs; subsequently change Product price/title/status. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.
- **Steps:** Repeat checkout with same ID set/key.

### STORE-001 — Public storefront visibility and identity

- **Feature area:** Public storefront and Creator storefront builder. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.

- **Steps:** Open /app/store/{creatorId}; GET /api/storefronts/{creatorId}.
- **Expected results:** Only Published Products exposed; public profile includes explicit publicEmail and never falls back to login email; image may be null. Public read requires Creator role to still exist.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.

### ADMIN-005 — Role change token invalidation scope

- **Feature area:** Administration, roles and audit. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P0 — platform administration boundary. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Admin with isolated synthetic accounts and Products. Target has access and refresh cookies issued before role update.
- **Steps:** Admin changes target role; target uses old access cookie, then attempts refresh and fresh login.
- **Expected results:** Refresh tokens deleted; expected result needs investigation for immediate permission revocation because JWT authorities come from unexpired token, while some services inspect current DB role.

## Creator Sales and order detail

### SALES-001 — Sales summary period matrix

- **Feature area:** Creator Sales and order detail. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Creator with own synthetic commerce history; another Creator for isolation. Orders across today/7d/30d/90d, retainedPAID, REFUNDED and FAILED timestamps.
- **Steps:** Open /app/sales; select each period; GET /api/creator/sales/summary?period={period}.
- **Expected results:** Retained paid revenue/count based on paidAt, refunds on refundedAt, failures on failedAt; UTC calendar window and equal previous period determine comparison, not local browser midnight.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.

### SALES-010 — Sales API validation

- **Feature area:** Creator Sales and order detail. **Test level:** API integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Creator with own synthetic commerce history; another Creator for isolation. Invalid period/status/sort, page-1,pageSize0/101,malformed Product ID. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.

Paid buyer, refunded buyer, free-enrolled/revoked-grant user, pending-only and failed-only users. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.

### ANALYTICS-014 — Reporting reconciliation journey

- **Feature area:** Creator Analytics and Dashboard. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — core user flow. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.

- **Steps:** Read Sales, Customers, Analytics and Dashboard before/after each supported operation using fixed UTC window.
- **Expected results:** Free enrollment adds customer/access without revenue; paid adds order/item revenue and access; failed affects payment metrics without alone creating customer; refund removes retained paid revenue and revokes matching access across views.

## Local notifications, fixture displays and absent client capabilities

### LOCAL-001 — Product notifications lifecycle

- **Feature area:** Local notifications, fixture displays and absent client capabilities. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** partial. **Priority:** P2 — honest state presentation. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Authenticated Creator; synthetic Product actions. Fresh Redux notification list.
- **Steps:** Create Product, update shared fields, add image, create/update section and lesson; open bell after each successful action; reload.
- **Expected results:** Fulfilled Product listeners append unread success notifications; reload clears session-only list; no notification backend persistence or delivery subscription.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.

### LOCAL-008 — Reviews client without backend routes

- **Feature area:** Local notifications, fixture displays and absent client capabilities. **Test level:** API integration. **Implementation state:** deliberately unavailable. **Priority:** P2 — honest state presentation. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Authenticated Creator; synthetic Product actions. Authenticated fixture with valid CSRF; arbitrary synthetic Product/review IDs. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.
- **Steps:** Attempt POST /api/products/{p}/review, PATCH /api/review/{r}, DELETE /api/reviews/{r}, GET /api/creators/reviews, PUT /api/review/{r}/visibility and POST/PUT/DELETE /api/review/{r}/reply using client DTO shapes.
- **Expected results:** These client functions have no matching backend controllers or reachable review management screen. No review/reply/visibility persistence should be claimed; exact unmapped-method response needs investigation.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.

- **Steps:** Toggle Collapse/Expand; navigate between management, storefront and landing builder; reload.
- **Expected results:** Sidebar follows layout context and requested builder collapse; account footer adapts. expected result needs investigation for persistence beyond implemented in-memory context, not assumed local-storage preference.

## Deployed dependencies, configuration and persistence boundaries

### ENV-001 — Deep-link hosting and base path

- **Feature area:** Deployed dependencies, configuration and persistence boundaries. **Test level:** deployed-service integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — deployment-dependent availability. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Synthetic authorized role fixtures; infrastructure harness only where explicitly stated. Deployed static frontend/backend and configured REACT_APP_BASE_PATH.
- **Steps:** Direct-load and reload /app/products/edit/{id}, /app/product/{id}, /app/store/{id}, /auth/verify-email?token={fixture}, /onboarding.
- **Expected results:** Hosting fallback serves SPA while preserving route/query; API base path resolves requests to backend rather than frontend index HTML. Legacy host redirects preserve path; deployed connection details remain external to catalog.
- **Required environment / external service:** Already-provisioned isolated deployment matching repository configuration; no deployment-specific addresses or secrets in case data.
- **Sources:** [application.yml](../../video-platform/src/main/resources/application.yml); [application-docs.yml](../../video-platform/src/main/resources/application-docs.yml); [SecurityConfig.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/configs/SecurityConfig.java); [SwaggerSecurityConfig.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/configs/SwaggerSecurityConfig.java); [db.changelog-master.xml](../../video-platform/src/main/resources/db/changelog/db.changelog-master.xml); [ci-cd-dokku.yml](../../video-platform/.github/workflows/ci-cd-dokku.yml); [_redirects](../../video-platform-ui/public/_redirects); [http-client.ts](../../video-platform-ui/src/core/api/http-client.ts)

- **Steps:** Open /docs/{syntheticPath} in isolated deployment.

### ENV-011 — Relational cascades and historical order retention

- **Feature area:** Deployed dependencies, configuration and persistence boundaries. **Test level:** API integration. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — deployment-dependent availability. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Synthetic authorized role fixtures; infrastructure harness only where explicitly stated. Disposable fixtures with quiz attempts, sections, Download metadata, native Membership content and refunded historical order; no active purchase/delete guard. Authentication prerequisite: use the stated role session cookies; mutations use matching XSRF-TOKEN cookie and X-XSRF-TOKEN header unless authentication/CSRF denial is the scenario.
- **Steps:** Delete content through supported owner endpoints; GET parent Product/feed/quiz and historical order/report APIs afterward.
- **Expected results:** Nested children/references disappear according to cascade mappings; historical immutable order items remain available where Product deletion is permitted. No direct DB query is required for observable expectations.
- **Required environment / external service:** Backend with migrated PostgreSQL test schema and disposable synthetic fixtures.

## Cross-feature journeys and recovery

### JOURNEY-001 — Free Course publishing to learning

- **Feature area:** Cross-feature journeys and recovery. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — end-to-end consistency. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Separate synthetic Guest/User, Creator owner, second Creator and Admin fixtures. Creator owns ready free Course; unrelated User has no grant.
- **Steps:** Creator publish, configure public landing/storefront; Guest discover; User enroll and read article; reload Library; Creator inspect Customers.
- **Expected results:** Only published metadata discoverable via public presentation; grant unlocks protected content and creates customer relationship without monetary sale; local progress is not invented.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.
- **Sources:** [app-router.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/routes/app-router.tsx); [product-page.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/pages/product-page/product-page.component.tsx); [cart.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/pages/normal-user-specific/cart/cart.component.tsx); [library-products.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/pages/normal-user-specific/library-page/library-products.component.tsx); [ProductService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/product/ProductService.java); [ProductEntitlementService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/entitlement/ProductEntitlementService.java); [CommercePaymentEventService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/commerce/CommercePaymentEventService.java); [CreatorReportingSupport.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/creator/CreatorReportingSupport.java)

### JOURNEY-002 — Paid Download to refund

- **Feature area:** Cross-feature journeys and recovery. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — end-to-end consistency. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Separate synthetic Guest/User, Creator owner, second Creator and Admin fixtures. Paid published Download with stored bytes; fake payments enabled in isolated environment.
- **Steps:** User discover/add cart/checkout PAID; open Library and download; Admin simulate fullrefund; User reload/read/download; Creator inspect Sales/Customers/Analytics.
- **Expected results:** Paid grants file access, refund revokes originating purchase access and affects reports; previously issued signed URL may remain valid until its expiry, so immediate object-URL revocation is not assumed.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.

### JOURNEY-006 — Logout/account-switch data isolation

- **Feature area:** Cross-feature journeys and recovery. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — end-to-end consistency. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Separate synthetic Guest/User, Creator owner, second Creator and Admin fixtures. UserA Library and CreatorA reporting fetched; separate UserB/CreatorB credentials.
- **Steps:** Logout then sign in B without full tab close; visit Library/reports/Product detail; reload.
- **Expected results:** Backend reads use B scope; expected result needs investigation for Redux-cached data while requests load. Browser-local cart/wishlist may persist but must not grant A access to B.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.
- **Sources:** [app-router.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/routes/app-router.tsx); [product-page.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/pages/product-page/product-page.component.tsx); [cart.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/pages/normal-user-specific/cart/cart.component.tsx); [library-products.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/pages/normal-user-specific/library-page/library-products.component.tsx); [ProductService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/product/ProductService.java); [ProductEntitlementService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/entitlement/ProductEntitlementService.java); [CommercePaymentEventService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/commerce/CommercePaymentEventService.java); [CreatorReportingSupport.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/creator/CreatorReportingSupport.java)

### JOURNEY-007 — Network interruption at persistence boundary

- **Feature area:** Cross-feature journeys and recovery. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — end-to-end consistency. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Separate synthetic Guest/User, Creator owner, second Creator and Admin fixtures. Unsaved Product edit, storefront draft and paid checkout in separate fixtures.
- **Steps:** Interrupt after request submission but before response; restore, reload server-backed resource and retry same operation/key.
- **Expected results:** Server reads establish actual commit state; retry checkout reuses idempotency; authoring cannot be assumed rolled back merely because response was lost.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.
- **Sources:** [app-router.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/routes/app-router.tsx); [product-page.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/pages/product-page/product-page.component.tsx); [cart.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/pages/normal-user-specific/cart/cart.component.tsx); [library-products.component.tsx](../../video-platform-ui/src/domains/app/pages/normal-user-specific/library-page/library-products.component.tsx); [ProductService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/product/ProductService.java); [ProductEntitlementService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/entitlement/ProductEntitlementService.java); [CommercePaymentEventService.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/commerce/CommercePaymentEventService.java); [CreatorReportingSupport.java](../../video-platform/src/main/java/com/myproject/video/video_platform/service/creator/CreatorReportingSupport.java)

### JOURNEY-008 — Unrelated-owner resource substitution

- **Feature area:** Cross-feature journeys and recovery. **Test level:** browser E2E. **Implementation state:** server-backed. **Priority:** P1 — end-to-end consistency. **Status:** Proposed; awaiting execution.
- **Role / starting state / prerequisites / test data:** Separate synthetic Guest/User, Creator owner, second Creator and Admin fixtures. Two Creators with Course/Download/Membership/config and customer/order records.
- **Steps:** Replace one path ID at a time with other owner's valid ID across authoring, download, landing, feed, customer and order calls.
- **Expected results:** Parent/owner/current-principal checks prevent cross-owner mutation/disclosure; Admin bypass only where explicitly implemented, never for Creator-only reports/config or foreign calendar disconnect.
- **Required environment / external service:** Test frontend and backend with persistent test data; no external service unless specified.
